# AI Media Scout — 2026-10-23

Expanded discovery covers 65 creative fields, 518 repository queries and 18 model tasks, with primary-source web research across all observed fields. Today’s guides complete earlier screened entries and add newly researched audio, animation, CAD, video, image and 3D workflows. First library coverage is distinguished from an actual new release. Complete software grants were reviewed; model, asset, paid-service and proprietary-host terms remain separate. These are documentation reviews, not installations or hands-on demonstrations. Independent quality evidence remains incomplete for most additions. The collector’s interrupted document phase was recovered from saved search responses without repeating searches; two repository queries failed, one was partial, and 112 were bounded by pagination limits. Search bounds and ecosystem gaps remain explicit. Weekly whole-library review begins October 26 and is not due today.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

## Coverage

| Field | Finding |
| --- | --- |
| Images & design | Researched generation, annotation-guided edits and restoration. AI Image Edit provides a mobile-oriented editor, but its default Qwen-Image-2.1 workflow has non-commercial model terms; this is separated from the open software license. [Source 1](https://discuss.huggingface.co/t/ai-image-edit-open-source-image-editing-app-with-a-mobile-friendly-ui/190778) [Source 2](https://v7.invoke.ai/) |
| Video, animation & film | Covered local generation, transcript editing, agent-assisted cutting and production planning. The JoyAI official model page was revisited; an unchanged already-profiled tool is not repeated as a new discovery. [Source 1](https://huggingface.co/jdopensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit) [Source 2](https://cutscript.dev/docs) |
| Audio, music & voice | Expanded into learned DSP export, neural amp inference, audiobook production and live music. ADAC connects differentiable training to real-time processing; author performance claims remain distinct from independent tests. [Source 1](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 2](https://magenta.tensorflow.org/magenta-realtime-2) |
| 3D, reconstruction & assets | Researched asset generation, geometric inference and scene reconstruction. Asset Tap documents a cross-platform provider-driven asset pipeline; generated geometry still needs topology, rights and downstream workflow review. [Source 1](https://assettap.dev/download/) [Source 2](https://gencad.github.io/) |
| Browser tools & web media | Browser-native creative tools and local web interfaces remain in scope. Khuwari offers local ML inbetweening. A browser UI alone does not establish support for every inference host. [Source 1](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) [Source 2](https://ml5js.org/) |
| WebXR, VR & AR | Immersive-web authoring and spatial AI workflows were researched through Meta’s developer material and related scene-generation projects. SDK availability is not a verified end-to-end VR creative demonstration. [Source 1](https://developers.meta.com/vr/blog/accelerate-vr-development-with-ai-and-immersive-web-sdk/) |
| Computational art & creative coding | Researched browser ML, learned DSP and custom creative pipelines. ml5 supplies artist-oriented ML examples; generic infrastructure or software merely written with AI is not automatically eligible. [Source 1](https://ml5js.org/) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) |
| Interactive, immersive & live media | Covered editable agent-controlled creative systems and live model interaction. LuminAI documents AI dance interaction, but a reviewed software grant was not established for a new eligible entry. [Source 1](https://expressivemachinery.gatech.edu/luminai/) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) |
| 3D printing & generative CAD | Searched CAD-to-object and AI craft workflows. ClayScape describes generative-AI/ceramic fabrication research; a paper or exported mesh is not proof of a licensed app or a print-ready design. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25657) [Source 2](https://agentcad.dev/) |
| Games & production pipelines | Covered editable game scenes, animation and asset generation. Asset Tap and LibreTerrain provide project-specific workflows; required providers, models and engine terms remain separate. [Source 1](https://assettap.dev/) [Source 2](https://libreterrain.org/docs/) |
| Motion capture & character animation | Researched artist-controlled inbetweening and agent-authored timelines. Khuwari explicitly starts from supplied keyframes; AI-authored finished pixels and editable scene operations are treated as different workflows. [Source 1](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) |
| Avatars, digital humans & lip sync | Researched digital presenters, facial animation and signing avatars. The Signature BSL project describes a capture/AI proof of concept; complete code-license eligibility remains unresolved. [Source 1](https://iet.open.ac.uk/projects/signature-bsl) |
| VFX, compositing & relighting | Relighting and neural-render proxy work supplemented repository searches. Disney’s project page describes interactive/differentiable lighting research; a reviewed released code grant was not established. [Source 1](https://studios.disneyresearch.com/2026/07/01/neural-render-proxies-for-interactive-and-differentiable-lighting/) [Source 2](https://neural-gaffer.github.io/) |
| Spatial audio & volumetric media | Researched spatial audio, neural scene representations and immersive environments. Audible World Models exposes research results but marks its code as coming soon, so it is not counted as available open-source software. [Source 1](https://cdwj.github.io/projects/audible-world-models/) [Source 2](https://semanticsplat.github.io/) |
| Photogrammetry, scanning & neural rendering | Covered monocular geometry, computational cameras and reconstruction. Metric3D receives a first full guide; current library inclusion is not a claim of a new launch or a calibrated measurement result. [Source 1](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/) [Source 2](https://waller-lab.github.io/LenslessLearning/index.html) |
| Editing, captions & post-production | Researched transcript cuts, captioning, voiceover and image refinement. Cutscript’s official documentation supplies a concrete editing workflow; service-dependent AI is distinguished from offline timeline editing. [Source 1](https://cutscript.dev/docs) [Source 2](https://jmsdevlab.com/tools/text2audio) |
| Vector graphics, illustration & textures | Covered technical SVG drawing, editable animation and gesture-driven design. HandType’s creator describes a MediaPipe hand-tracking typography tool, but no complete root software license was found in the inspected repository. [Source 1](https://www.justinwetch.com/blog/handtype) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) |
| Typography, fonts & layout | Researched hand-tracked letterform control and kinetic typography. HandType remains a license gap; a compelling creative demonstration does not substitute for an open-source grant. [Source 1](https://www.justinwetch.com/blog/handtype) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38325) |
| Storyboarding, narrative & comics | StoryForge’s live documentation describes fiction, screenplay/comic adaptation and world development. Its showcase disables generation, and several interactive products remain previews; those differences are reflected in the guide. [Source 1](https://docs.storyforge-lab.com/en/) |
| Creative publishing & presentation | Covered slides, narrative packages and chaptered audiobook output. Text2Audio’s local workflow is documented, with non-commercial F5-TTS weights separated from MIT application code. [Source 1](https://jmsdevlab.com/tools/text2audio) [Source 2](https://docs.storyforge-lab.com/en/) |
| Photography, restoration & color | Researched semantic photo organization, depth references and restoration. Pik-Fix supplies primary research context; image examples do not establish fidelity or permission to use every model commercially. [Source 1](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/WACV2023/papers/Xu_Pik-Fix_Restoring_and_Colorizing_Old_Photos_WACV_2023_paper.pdf) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/) |
| Data art & scientific visualization | Researched model-assisted diagramming and data/media visualization. ml5 and AIiveVR provide primary creative/educational context. Semiotic’s actual license file contains an added approval sentence and is held pending clarification. [Source 1](https://ml5js.org/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.15193) |
| Physical, robotic & kinetic installations | Interactive dance, body/sensor-driven art and multisensory installations were searched. LuminAI and Mind Draw Play describe creative systems; availability and complete software grants require their own review. [Source 1](https://expressivemachinery.gatech.edu/luminai/) [Source 2](https://www.neur0forest.art/mind-draw-play) |
| Performance, projection & stage media | Live music and interactive generation were researched alongside audio-plugin authoring. Magenta’s official material documents real-time music work; no live performance or latency test was run here. [Source 1](https://magenta.tensorflow.org/magenta-realtime-2) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/) |
| Fashion, textiles & wearable media | Covered garment recognition and design-to-pattern research. Design2GarmentCode describes a multimodal sewing-pattern workflow; Pasma’s separate local garment classifier is reviewed for user-correctable visual suggestions. [Source 1](https://style3d.github.io/design2garmentcode/) |
| Accessible media & assistive creation | Researched narration, captioning and signed communication. Text2Audio is relevant to alternate media access, while Signature BSL remains a research/code-availability gap. These are creative workflow observations, not accessibility certification. [Source 1](https://jmsdevlab.com/tools/text2audio) [Source 2](https://iet.open.ac.uk/projects/signature-bsl) |
| Mobile, edge & on-device creation | Covered Android creative control and mobile browser editing. The AI Image Edit author’s announcement describes a phone-oriented UI; inference still runs on a separate supported GPU host. [Source 1](https://discuss.huggingface.co/t/ai-image-edit-open-source-image-editing-app-with-a-mobile-friendly-ui/190778) |
| Creative learning & authoring | Artist-facing ML and inspectable animation workflows were researched. ml5’s examples support learning creative ML; no classroom efficacy claims are inferred. [Source 1](https://ml5js.org/) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) |
| Archives, media restoration & collections | Researched restoration, searchable photo archives and audio reuse. Pik-Fix gives a primary restoration reference; generated restoration can alter content and is not archival ground truth. [Source 1](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/WACV2023/papers/Xu_Pik-Fix_Restoring_and_Colorizing_Old_Photos_WACV_2023_paper.pdf) |
| Emerging & cross-disciplinary creative AI | Searched adjacent practices beyond the starter examples, including learned DSP compilation, sensory installations and neural material authoring. A new field does not relax the code-license or concrete-AI-use checks. [Source 1](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 2](https://www.media.mit.edu/projects/anemoia-device/overview/) |
| Tactile, vibration and haptic media | HapticGen’s project page describes text-conditioned vibration generation and its research setup. No new complete software-license/installation review was established for a separate haptic tool today. [Source 1](https://hapticgen.hcitech.org/) |
| Neural acoustics and responsive sound spaces | Covered learned room/audio representations and spatial sound research. Audible World Models still marks code coming soon; acoustic-rendering repositories receive separate license and dependency screening. [Source 1](https://cdwj.github.io/projects/audible-world-models/) |
| AI agents for code-authored media production | Researched agent-directed video, design and narrative preparation. StoryForge distinguishes deliverable reference packages from actual finished video production, which informs the scope of its guide. [Source 1](https://docs.storyforge-lab.com/en/) |
| AI kinetic typography and animated lettering | Researched typographic motion and editable agent-driven animation. Strike a Chord supplies a primary kinetic-typography paper; a reviewed reusable software release was not established from that paper alone. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38325) |
| AI choreography and dance composition | LuminAI documents movement improvisation with a virtual partner. Gesture-generation code was screened separately; dance documentation does not by itself establish an open software grant. [Source 1](https://expressivemachinery.gatech.edu/luminai/) |
| AI-assisted textile and computational craft | Searched embroidery, garments and generated craft geometry. Design2GarmentCode is a relevant primary project; Threadspool was held because its inspected root lacked a complete license file. [Source 1](https://style3d.github.io/design2garmentcode/) |
| AI music notation and score recovery | Muscriptor’s official site was researched for score transcription. Existing notation tools are not recycled solely to fill a category; new eligible additions require complete code and model-term checks. [Source 1](https://muscriptor.kyutai.org/) |
| Neural relighting and material recovery | Covered inverse rendering, relightable assets and video-lighting papers. NeAR and PIXL-Relight provide primary research context; benchmark claims do not establish support for a user’s specific scene. [Source 1](https://near-project.github.io/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18735) |
| Olfactory and multisensory AI media | Researched smell-conditioned media and AI scent installations. Anemoia Device and Microsoft’s olfaction research are primary findings; no fully reviewed creator-facing open-source release is claimed today. [Source 1](https://www.media.mit.edu/projects/anemoia-device/overview/) [Source 2](https://microsoft.github.io/olfaction/) |
| AI architectural visualization and spatial design | Covered scene, CAD and terrain generation. ArtiCAD provides a primary assembly-generation paper; agent-assisted terrain authoring adds a directly documented creative workflow without certifying structural correctness. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10992) [Source 2](https://libreterrain.org/docs/) |
| Neural holography and light-field media | Searched learned holography and display-oriented scene representations. Current primary publications and institutional records were found, but a separately reviewed software grant remains a gap. [Source 1](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41566-026-02016-9) [Source 2](https://www.repository.cam.ac.uk/items/29466216-8196-4b78-a02f-6d154f776451) |
| Neural rigging and digital puppetry | Researched editable character motion, keyframe interpolation and facial/gesture pipelines. Khuwari’s documented keyframe workflow is an adjacent primary finding; it is not treated as a complete puppet-rigging system. [Source 1](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) |
| AI ceramics and archaeological illustration | ClayScape and earlier AI-aided sculpture research were checked for ceramic design/fabrication. Research results do not establish a downloadable, licensed app or a tested print process. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25657) [Source 2](https://var-mar.info/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/AIaidedSculptures_EvoMusArt-proceedings_OP.pdf) |
| AI parametric design and jewelry | Researched AI-directed CAD and geometry workflows. AgentCAD and GenCAD supplied live primary context; new guides distinguish editable CAD concepts from engineering validation. [Source 1](https://agentcad.dev/) [Source 2](https://gencad.github.io/) |
| AI cartography and artistic maps | RustGIS and GeoLibre were researched as mapping interfaces with AI assistance. Their complete software/model grants and reproducible creative workflows were not fully established for a new eligible entry. [Source 1](https://rustgis.app/) [Source 2](https://geolibre.app/) |
| AI robotic painting and drawing | Checked a primary image-to-painting robotics project and adjacent AI sculpture work. Robotic actuation or an AI-assisted development process alone is not enough to establish the required creative AI functionality. [Source 1](https://artinkeringlab.iitj.ac.in/projects/bob-ross-without-ros) [Source 2](https://var-mar.info/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/AIaidedSculptures_EvoMusArt-proceedings_OP.pdf) |
| AI cel animation and inbetweening | Khuwari and ToonComposer’s official project materials supplemented searches. The guide emphasizes artist-authored frames and editable inbetweening; no app was installed or rendered in this review. [Source 1](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) [Source 2](https://lg-li.github.io/project/tooncomposer/) |
| AI Foley and sound effects | Searched audio generation tied to visual scenes and spatial events. Audible World Models offers research demonstrations but no released code yet, which is retained as an availability gap. [Source 1](https://cdwj.github.io/projects/audible-world-models/) |
| Learned media codecs and neural delivery | Kyutai’s codec explainer supplied technical context, and codec repositories were screened for speech/media use. A released codec is distinguished from an unreleased full dialogue system. [Source 1](https://kyutai.org/codec-explainer/) |
| AI assisted sound data art and corpus instruments | Researched neural-model visualization/sonification and sensor-driven art. AIiveVR is a primary research reference; ordinary sonification without an established AI component is not automatically included. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2109.15193) [Source 2](https://www.neur0forest.art/mind-draw-play) |
| AI biosignal art and neurointeractive media | Mind Draw Play and related EEG creative systems were researched. Biosignal input itself does not prove AI functionality, and a project description is not a complete software license. [Source 1](https://www.neur0forest.art/mind-draw-play) [Source 2](https://nouscope.org/) |
| Neural procedural material authoring | NeuMatEx describes neural material authoring with inverse rendering. Its project page exposes paper/results material but no reviewed downloadable software grant, so it remains a gap. [Source 1](https://nvlabs.github.io/neumatex/) |
| Neural cameras and unconventional image capture | Researched lensless imaging and learned camera reconstruction using university project pages. These remain source-backed research findings, not inferred hardware compatibility or new app releases. [Source 1](https://waller-lab.github.io/LenslessLearning/index.html) [Source 2](https://www.ee.iitm.ac.in/comp_photolab/) [Source 3](https://iilab.io/research.html) |
| AI origami, kirigami and paper engineering | COrigami supplied a primary AI-assisted flat-folding research lead. No complete reviewed creator-tool release was established; mathematical results are not a print/cut workflow guarantee. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299) |
| AI landscape, garden and environmental design | LibreTerrain was found through its official documentation and GitLab source. Its local MCP bridge lets an assistant edit terrain graphs, with platform package availability disclosed separately. [Source 1](https://libreterrain.org/docs/) [Source 2](https://gitlab.com/libreterrain/libreterrain) |
| Neural mosaic and stained-glass media | Researched neural mosaic practices using an artist’s project page and a CVF art record. Artificial-Art source was found but had no complete root license in the inspected files, so it is held. [Source 1](https://salavon.com/work/genmo/grid/58/) [Source 2](https://www.thecvf-art.com/project.php?artist=ashwin-nedungadi&id=933&year=2026) |
| AI optical illusions and anamorphic media | Visual Anagrams supplied a primary perceptual-image generation reference. Existing work is not treated as newly released; any separate software/model restrictions still apply. [Source 1](https://dangeng.github.io/visual_anagrams/) [Source 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.17919) |
| AI relief engraving and learned halftoning | Researched learned halftoning as an adjacent image-to-mark workflow. The paper supports the research finding, not a verified engraving-machine integration or physical output. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.11408) |
| Neural brushstroke painting and drawn media | Searched neural brush and robotic-painting workflows. Two GitHub queries in this field failed and a third was partial because its next-page response was unavailable after the interrupted collection; these remain explicit coverage gaps. Supplemental live primary-source search found StrokeDiff brushstroke-generation research and the IIT Jodhpur physical-painting project; neither finding establishes a newly available open-source implementation without a complete code/license review. [Source 1](https://artinkeringlab.iitj.ac.in/projects/bob-ross-without-ros) [Source 2](https://doi.org/10.1145/3822182) |
| AI color direction and palette-based media | Adaptive Color Grading supplies a current primary paper on automated color workflows. It is retained as research context until a complete software release and model terms are verified. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21169) |
| AI collage, cut-paper composition and layered media | Collage Diffusion’s official project and paper were checked for layer composition and harmonization. This established work is not recycled as a new release. [Source 1](https://vsanimator.github.io/collage_diffusion/) [Source 2](https://openaccess.thecvf.com/content/WACV2024/html/Sarukkai_Collage_Diffusion_WACV_2024_paper.html) |
| AI stereo conversion and spatial video authoring | Researched learned display radiance fields and neural stereo/display representations. The primary paper supports the research finding; hardware display support and an eligible app release remain unverified. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03356) |
| AI semantic editing of captured 3D scenes | SemanticSplat’s project page was checked for language-aware Gaussian scene representations. Today’s geometric pipelines are assessed individually; a semantic research result is not a universal scene-editing capability. [Source 1](https://semanticsplat.github.io/) |
| AI signing avatars and expressive gesture media | Signature BSL and TERP 360 materials were researched for expressive signing avatars. Public demonstrations and awards do not establish a reviewed open-source software grant. [Source 1](https://iet.open.ac.uk/projects/signature-bsl) [Source 2](https://ars.electronica.art/starts-prize/en/terp-360-embodied-ai-for-expressive-sign-language/) |
| AI assisted audio plugin and DSP authoring | New adaptive field: AI-assisted audio-plugin authoring and compilation of learned DSP. Ruse and ADAC received source/license review; their plugin targets, dependencies and unverified quality checks are explicit. [Source 1](https://www.ruse.audio/) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/) |

## Search and review counts

65 creative fields; 518/518 repository queries attempted; 18/18 model-task queries attempted. 25958 distinct source candidates and 854 model leads. 52 detailed profiles and 28 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 2 failed repository queries, 1 partial repository queries, 112 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 2 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: Inspected live GitLab topic results and LibreTerrain source documentation. Archived and read its complete MIT option. The terrain-authoring tool already has a full library guide, so it is not recycled as a new addition. No other eligible new GitLab release was established in this pass. [Source](https://gitlab.com/libreterrain/libreterrain) [Source](https://libreterrain.org/docs/)
- **codeberg** · gap: The live site-scoped search was blocked by robots restrictions. No Codeberg-wide coverage or negative finding is claimed.
- **sourcehut** · gap: The live git.sr.ht search was blocked by robots restrictions. This ecosystem remains a recorded access gap.
- **packages** · searched: Reviewed ADAC’s PyPI package and official documentation and followed package install references for creative tools. Package metadata was supplemented by complete source-license reviews. [Source](https://pypi.org/project/adac/) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Researched audio-plugin authoring plus native DCC and animation bridges. Proprietary host licenses, external agents and model rights remain separate from open bridge code. [Source](https://www.ruse.audio/) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source](https://github.com/pouulll/after-effects-mcp-extended)
- **project-sites** · searched: Live project-site research included Khuwari, Text2Audio, Asset Tap, LibreTerrain and multiple sensor/creative research sites. Product claims were checked against repository documentation and licenses where included. [Source](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html) [Source](https://jmsdevlab.com/tools/text2audio) [Source](https://assettap.dev/download/) [Source](https://libreterrain.org/docs/)
- **research-code** · searched: Primary research covered relighting, materials, holography, typography, craft, color and scene generation. Papers and code-coming-soon projects are disclosed separately from available licensed tools. [Source](https://nvlabs.github.io/neumatex/) [Source](https://cdwj.github.io/projects/audible-world-models/) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21169) [Source](https://style3d.github.io/design2garmentcode/)
- **international** · searched: Reviewed Chinese-language creator workflows and multilingual/Korean project documentation, including StoryForge, mapedit, Dashi and AIMovieStorage. Language and geography did not set eligibility or quality tiers. [Source](https://docs.storyforge-lab.com/) [Source](https://github.com/karylcode/mapedit) [Source](https://github.com/raonolje/AIMovieStorage) [Source](https://github.com/chuspeeism/dashi-ppt-skill)

## Ruse

Audio, music & voice · AI assisted audio plugin and DSP authoring

First complete library guide for a repository created within the current search window; creation date is not proof of a product launch.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Prototyping custom effects such as saturators, de-essers, compressors and sidechain processors for a DAW.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-23. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README includes ten example effects and explains the automated plugin checks; these are author demonstrations, not our audio tests. [Source](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.ruse.audio/)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the source, install the local Rust MCP crate, create a plugin workspace and configure the supported agent client. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.ruse.audio/)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** No generative audio weights are bundled. The selected coding agent and its model have separate terms. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.ruse.audio/)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Public beta 0.2.0; MIDI/instrument packaging, VST3, several DSP families and other platform packaging are unfinished. Automated checks do not establish musical quality.

### How it uses AI

Ruse lets an AI coding agent assemble Rust audio effects through an MCP server and a documented DSP library. [Source](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### Introduction

Ruse lets an AI coding agent assemble Rust audio effects through an MCP server and a documented DSP library. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### What it is good for

Prototyping custom effects such as saturators, de-essers, compressors and sidechain processors for a DAW. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### Demo & examples

The README includes ten example effects and explains the automated plugin checks; these are author demonstrations, not our audio tests. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### Install

Clone the source, install the local Rust MCP crate, create a plugin workspace and configure the supported agent client. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Clone the source, install the local Rust MCP crate, create a plugin workspace and configure the supported agent client.

```sh
git clone https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse
```


```sh
cd ruse
```


```sh
cargo install --path crates/mcp
```


```sh
ruse-mcp new ~/my-plugins
```


```sh
ruse-mcp setup claude-desktop
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

1. Describe the effect and its controls to the connected agent.
2. Build the generated plugin and inspect the reported checks.
3. Load the CLAP or AU effect in a compatible DAW and audition it on representative audio.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Stable Rust; an MCP-capable agent; compatible CLAP/AU host. OS minimum is not documented.
- **Platforms:** macOS packaging documented; Windows and Linux packaging unfinished

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://www.ruse.audio/)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** No generative audio weights are bundled. The selected coding agent and its model have separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. No generative audio weights are bundled. The selected coding agent and its model have separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### Limitations

Public beta 0.2.0; MIDI/instrument packaging, VST3, several DSP families and other platform packaging are unfinished. Automated checks do not establish musical quality. [Source 1](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://www.ruse.audio/)

### Get the tool

- [Documentation](https://github.com/Symbia-Labs/ruse/blob/main/README.md)

## ADAC

Audio, music & voice · AI assisted audio plugin and DSP authoring

First complete guide in this library, including any earlier screened entry. This is new library coverage, not a claim that the software launched today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Turning learned reverb and other differentiable DSP designs into inspectable real-time processing code and plugin projects.

Evidence checked: 2026-10-23. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Official documentation demonstrates a feedback-delay-network reverb and export/equivalence checks. Its performance claims are author measurements. [Source](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Install the editable package from the repository; use the full extra for FLAMO and PyTorch. Install the separate FAUST/JUCE toolchain when exporting plugins. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.
- **Independent use · unknown:** Independent first-hand use or a working third-party integration was not established for this selected workflow.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** User-trained model parameters are separate from the MIT compiler. FLAMO, FAUST and JUCE retain their own terms. Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Supported graph operations and export targets constrain what can be compiled. No independent reproduction was established.

### How it uses AI

ADAC compiles trained differentiable audio processors from FLAMO/PyTorch into a JSON intermediate representation and FAUST DSP. [Source](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

### Introduction

ADAC compiles trained differentiable audio processors from FLAMO/PyTorch into a JSON intermediate representation and FAUST DSP. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

### What it is good for

Turning learned reverb and other differentiable DSP designs into inspectable real-time processing code and plugin projects. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

### Demo & examples

Official documentation demonstrates a feedback-delay-network reverb and export/equivalence checks. Its performance claims are author measurements. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

### Install

Install the editable package from the repository; use the full extra for FLAMO and PyTorch. Install the separate FAUST/JUCE toolchain when exporting plugins. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the editable package from the repository; use the full extra for FLAMO and PyTorch. Install the separate FAUST/JUCE toolchain when exporting plugins.

```sh
pip install -e .
```


```sh
pip install -e ".[full]"
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

1. Train or load a supported FLAMO model.
2. Export it with adac.flamo_to_faust(model, fs=48000, name="MyReverb").
3. Inspect the generated DSP and validate the output before building a plugin.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/cucuwritescode/adac/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://adac.readthedocs.io/en/latest/) [Source 3](https://pypi.org/project/adac/)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.10+; NumPy for the core; FLAMO/PyTorch for model export; FAUST and JUCE for the documented plugin path.
- **Platforms:** Python package; macOS JUCE build/install example documented; other plugin targets require separate verification

### How it uses AI

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Without installed model weights the service can fall back to a deterministic fake adapter, which is not evidence of AI generation. Autonomous-agent and mixing experiments are deferred.

### How it uses AI

Mosh combines a native DAW with a local generative service that transforms a selected audio clip from a prompt. [Source](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

Mosh combines a native DAW with a local generative service that transforms a selected audio clip from a prompt. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Trying prompted timbre changes inside an arrangement, then auditioning and keeping or rejecting each generated clip. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README shows an arrangement session and identifies Direct SA3 Re-Imagine as the current supported development scope. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Use the signed Apple Silicon release or build and launch with the repository wrapper; set up the Stable Audio service separately. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the signed Apple Silicon release or build and launch with the repository wrapper; set up the Stable Audio service separately.

```sh
./run-mosh.sh build
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

1. Import or record a short clip.
2. Select it, enter a transformation prompt and generate.
3. Audition against the original and choose Keep or Reject.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Native C++/JUCE/Tracktion engine, React UI and Python/MLX generative service; Stable Audio 3 setup is separate.
- **Platforms:** macOS Apple Silicon canonical; Windows NVIDIA/CUDA port documented; Linux GUI unsupported

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** AGPL-3.0
- **Weights:** Stable Audio 3 model terms apply separately. JUCE, Tracktion and other components retain their licenses. Optional agent APIs have separate terms.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows AGPL-3.0. Stable Audio 3 model terms apply separately. JUCE, Tracktion and other components retain their licenses. Optional agent APIs have separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute; optional external agent APIs may charge separately.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Without installed model weights the service can fall back to a deterministic fake adapter, which is not evidence of AI generation. Autonomous-agent and mixing experiments are deferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/esanchezharris/Mosh/blob/main/README.md)

Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate. [Source](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Wrong focal length distorts point clouds. Rare objects and aerial views can be unreliable; a point cloud is not a print-ready mesh.

### How it uses AI

Metric3D predicts metric depth, and Metric3Dv2 also predicts surface normals, from one photograph. [Source](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

### Introduction

Metric3D predicts metric depth, and Metric3Dv2 also predicts surface normals, from one photograph. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

### What it is good for

Creating depth and normal references for scene reconstruction, compositing or camera-aware 3D experiments. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

### Demo & examples

The official project includes image-to-depth/normal and point-cloud demonstrations plus published benchmark tables. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

### Install

Install the requirements for the chosen model family, download its checkpoint and use the repository inference script or PyTorch Hub interface. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the requirements for the chosen model family, download its checkpoint and use the repository inference script or PyTorch Hub interface.

```sh
pip install -r requirements_v2.txt
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

1. Choose a ViT checkpoint and supply an image.
2. Set camera intrinsics/focal length correctly for metric reconstruction.
3. Inspect depth, normals and point-cloud geometry before importing into a creative pipeline.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python/PyTorch with requirements_v2.txt for ViT or requirements_v1.txt for ConvNeXt; optional ONNX runtime path.
- **Platforms:** Python and ONNX workflows; a complete supported-OS matrix is not documented

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

- **Code:** BSD-2-Clause
- **Weights:** Root code is BSD-2-Clause. Checkpoint, pretrained backbone and dataset terms must be checked separately; the README directs commercial inquiries to the authors.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows BSD-2-Clause. Root code is BSD-2-Clause. Checkpoint, pretrained backbone and dataset terms must be checked separately; the README directs commercial inquiries to the authors.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

### Limitations

Wrong focal length distorts point clouds. Rare objects and aerial views can be unreliable; a point cloud is not a print-ready mesh. [Source 1](https://github.com/YvanYin/Metric3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://jugghm.github.io/Metric3Dv2/)

- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use a platform release, or clone with submodules, run workspace setup and start the Electron developer app. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** A release or active repository alone does not establish sustained support. Workflow-specific support and unresolved blockers need a deeper review.

- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Every image/video model and provider has separate terms. The MIT studio license does not cover their weights or outputs. Local compute and storage, or charged Muapi/provider requests; a cloud key is optional for local-only use. [Source](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Local inference is absent from the hosted website. Wan2GP has no Apple MPS path; Mac clients can connect to a supported GPU server. The author warns Z-Image can hang an 8 GB Mac.

### How it uses AI

Open Generative AI is a desktop/web studio for image and video generation, with cloud providers and desktop-only local inference engines. [Source](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

Open Generative AI is a desktop/web studio for image and video generation, with cloud providers and desktop-only local inference engines. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Managing image/video experiments and multi-step workflows in one interface, including local sd.cpp images or a separately installed Wan2GP backend. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The README documents image, video, lip-sync and node-workflow studios with model-specific controls. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Use a platform release, or clone with submodules, run workspace setup and start the Electron developer app. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use a platform release, or clone with submodules, run workspace setup and start the Electron developer app.

```sh
git clone --recurse-submodules https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI.git
```


```sh
cd Open-Generative-AI
```


```sh
npm run setup
```


```sh
npm run electron:dev
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

1. Choose cloud or local mode.
2. For local images, download a supported model; for video, configure and check a separate Wan2GP installation.
3. Generate a short test and inspect quality before a larger workflow.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Author recommends 16 GB RAM for sd.cpp Z-Image and describes Wan 2.1 1.3B at about 8 GB VRAM. Requirements vary by model; these are not universal minimums.
- **Software:** Source: Node.js 18+. sd.cpp is bundled in desktop builds; Wan2GP requires its separate Python/GPU environment.
- **Platforms:** macOS Intel/Apple Silicon desktop; Windows desktop; Linux desktop; Hosted web uses cloud APIs

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** Every image/video model and provider has separate terms. The MIT studio license does not cover their weights or outputs.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. Every image/video model and provider has separate terms. The MIT studio license does not cover their weights or outputs.
- **Cost:** Local compute and storage, or charged Muapi/provider requests; a cloud key is optional for local-only use.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Local inference is absent from the hosted website. Wan2GP has no Apple MPS path; Mac clients can connect to a supported GPU server. The author warns Z-Image can hang an 8 GB Mac. [Source 1](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/Anil-matcha/Open-Generative-AI/blob/main/README.md)

Khuwari uses machine-learning interpolation to fill gaps between hand-drawn keyframes inside the browser. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

### What it is good for

Making rough animation and game-art motion from artist-authored frames, with control over timing and interpolation per gap. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

### Demo & examples

The official site demonstrates a timeline, paint tools, color layers, camera controls and video/GIF/frame-sequence export. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

### Install

Open the hosted editor, or serve the downloaded source as a static site. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Open the hosted editor, or serve the downloaded source as a static site.

```sh
python3 -m http.server 4000
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

1. Draw or import keyframes.
2. Place them on the timeline and choose ML, squash/stretch or no interpolation for each gap.
3. Review generated transitions and export; save the editable .khuwari project.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Web browser; Python 3 only for the optional local static server. Node/Chromium are for development tests, not stated user requirements.
- **Platforms:** Browser; numerical browser-version and OS minimums are not documented

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

- **Code:** AGPL-3.0
- **Weights:** AGPL-3.0 application. Third-party interpolation models and libraries have separate credits; complete model-term verification remains open.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows AGPL-3.0. AGPL-3.0 application. Third-party interpolation models and libraries have separate credits; complete model-term verification remains open.
- **Cost:** Local browser compute; no cloud inference account is described.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

### Limitations

The author positions ML as inbetweening assistance for supplied artwork. Complex transitions still need review; claims about weak hardware are not a published minimum. [Source 1](https://github.com/TheShovel/khuwari/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://theshovel.rocks/khuwari/index.html)

### How it uses AI

AutoCAD MCP lets an AI assistant inspect, modify, dimension and export CAD drawings through a live AutoCAD bridge or headless DXF engine. [Source](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

AutoCAD MCP lets an AI assistant inspect, modify, dimension and export CAD drawings through a live AutoCAD bridge or headless DXF engine. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Producing technical drawings, layout studies and vector presentation sheets with reviewable coordinates and dimensions. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The repository shows piping, tank and site-layout sheets; these previews are approximate ezdxf/matplotlib renders, not AutoCAD captures. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install the repository package in a Python environment, copy the example configuration and connect the module as an MCP server. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the repository package in a Python environment, copy the example configuration and connect the module as an MCP server.

```sh
python -m pip install -e ".[dev]"
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

1. Confirm active drawing, units and the permitted workspace.
2. Ask the agent to inspect or create a DXF and make a scoped edit.
3. Audit dimensions and export PDF/PNG/SVG; use live AutoCAD only for supported DWG operations.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.11+ 64-bit; MCP SDK 1.x; ezdxf/matplotlib. Live COM needs matching AutoCAD architecture and user privileges.
- **Platforms:** Windows for live AutoCAD/Plant 3D; Any OS documented for headless DXF

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/C48H74/autocad-mcp/blob/main/README.md)

- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT bridge code; a licensed proprietary AutoCAD/Plant 3D host is required for live DWG work. The external agent has separate terms.
- **Cost:** Local compute and agent usage; live mode additionally needs an Autodesk license.

```sh
pnpm install
```

```sh
pnpm build
```


```sh
pnpm mapedit init ./my-maps
```

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/raonolje/AIMovieStorage/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** NVIDIA CUDA GPU required for the specified local generation paths. Exact RAM/VRAM/storage varies by engine and is not a universal published minimum.
- **Software:** Windows 10/11 x64 and WebView2. NVIDIA CUDA for GPU generation and SeedVR2; Node/pnpm/Rust only for source builds.
- **Platforms:** Windows x64; macOS and Linux currently unsupported

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/raonolje/AIMovieStorage/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/raonolje/AIMovieStorage/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT application; downloaded model weights have separate licenses. Optional Claude/OpenAI analysis and prompt-writing APIs have separate charges.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT application; downloaded model weights have separate licenses. Optional Claude/OpenAI analysis and prompt-writing APIs have separate charges.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### How it uses AI

MIDI-3D uses multi-instance diffusion to generate separate 3D scene objects and their spatial relationships from a segmented image. [Source](https://github.com/VAST-AI-Research/MIDI-3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://huanngzh.github.io/MIDI-Page/)

### Introduction

MIDI-3D uses multi-instance diffusion to generate separate 3D scene objects and their spatial relationships from a segmented image. [Source 1](https://github.com/VAST-AI-Research/MIDI-3D/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://huanngzh.github.io/MIDI-Page/)





### Introduction

mnft uses a language model to write CAD code, executes it through selectable geometry engines and exposes a browser preview plus STEP/STL output. [Source 1](https://github.com/nanassound/mnft/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Building a self-hosted conversational CAD prototype or integrating text-to-part authoring into an Elixir application. [Source 1](https://github.com/nanassound/mnft/blob/main/README.md)

The repository documents a reference Phoenix chat/viewer, a headless integration and pluggable build123d/OpenSCAD engines. [Source 1](https://github.com/nanassound/mnft/blob/main/README.md)

Prepare Elixir/Erlang, Python and uv, create the CAD worker environment, configure a model provider, and start the reference Phoenix app. A Docker route is also documented. [Source 1](https://github.com/nanassound/mnft/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Prepare Elixir/Erlang, Python and uv, create the CAD worker environment, configure a model provider, and start the reference Phoenix app. A Docker route is also documented.

```sh
cd cad_worker
```


```sh
uv venv --python 3.12
```


```sh
uv pip install -e . pytest
```


```sh
cd ../mnft_cad_web
```


```sh
mix setup
```


```sh
mix phx.server
```

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/nanassound/mnft/blob/main/README.md)

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **OpenDesign** · needs-license-review: The complete Apache-form license text changes clauses 6 and 9. Its SPDX label does not resolve whether this modified grant matches an approved license; held for clarification. [Source](https://github.com/nexu-io/open-design/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Semiotic** · needs-license-review: The license file adds a request to get project-maintainer approval for intended use. That extra sentence needs clarification before an unrestricted software eligibility claim. [Source](https://github.com/nteract/semiotic)
- **HandType** · needs-license-review: The repository license endpoint returned 404 and root inspection did not establish a complete software grant. The creator demo alone does not establish open-source eligibility. [Source](https://github.com/justinwetch/HandType)
- **Threadspool** · needs-license-review: No complete software license was collected from the repository endpoint or root inspection. Retained privately for further review. [Source](https://github.com/alexandra03/threadspool)
- **Artificial-Art** · needs-license-review: The repository license endpoint returned 404 and no complete root grant was established. [Source](https://github.com/pearsonkyle/Artificial-Art)
- **MSEmbGAN** · needs-license-review: Both README and license collection returned 404; a usable, licensed embroidery implementation was not established. [Source](https://github.com/CGVSL-NCKU/TVCG_MSEmbGAN)
- **AVR / Versa** · needs-license-review: The official neural acoustic-field repository has a documented research workflow but its license endpoint returned 404. A complete software grant remains unestablished. [Source](https://github.com/penn-waves-lab/AVR)
- **SAGE** · needs-license-review: The root Apache-2.0 grant does not settle the embedded M2T2 component under a separate NVIDIA license. The full selected pipeline remains held pending component-scope review. [Source](https://github.com/NVlabs/sage)
- **FastGS** · needs-license-review: The MIT root sits beside explicit instructions to follow separate upstream 3DGS, Taming 3DGS and SpeedySplat terms. The existing pending entry is not upgraded to a complete guide. [Source](https://github.com/fastgs/fastgs)
- **SUPRA-Tex** · needs-license-review: The original code is MIT, but bundled deployment source includes DINOv3 under a custom agreement. Its own notices explicitly distinguish that source from MIT/Apache components; the complete pipeline remains held. Qwen-derived material weights also have noncommercial terms. [Source](https://github.com/Supra-3D/SUPRA-Tex/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md)
- **motionmaxxing** · needs-license-review: Original tools are Apache-2.0, but the bundled GSAP runtime is explicitly labeled non-open-source in NOTICE. The mixed-code distribution needs a scoped eligibility review before inclusion. [Source](https://github.com/Tejashmakwana/motionmaxxing/blob/main/NOTICE)
- **Imagination Studio** · needs-license-review: Root Apache-2.0 text is standard, but NOTICE requires attribution for use and in both documentation and any about surface. The scope of that wording needs clarification; model restrictions and optional proprietary tools are also separate. [Source](https://github.com/drowzeys/Imagination-Studio-by-keys-Wasserman/blob/main/NOTICE)
- **CapRocket** · needs-license-review: The current repository metadata does not identify a software license and the license endpoint returned 404. A complete grant was not established, so the project is not counted as an eligible addition. [Source](https://github.com/medhachim/CapRocket)
- **Scene-Designer** · needs-license-review: The current repository metadata does not identify a software license and the license endpoint returned 404. A complete grant was not established, so the project is not counted as an eligible addition. [Source](https://github.com/ZJU-OmniAI/Scene-Designer)
- **Tetris3D** · needs-license-review: The current repository metadata does not identify a software license and the license endpoint returned 404. A complete grant was not established, so the project is not counted as an eligible addition. [Source](https://github.com/cvlab-kaist/Tetris3D)
- **InfiniDream** · needs-license-review: The current repository metadata does not identify a software license and the license endpoint returned 404. A complete grant was not established, so the project is not counted as an eligible addition. [Source](https://github.com/yichuanH/InfiniDream)
- **RE-USE model** · excluded: The current model card describes the original model as research/development only under NVIDIA’s noncommercial license. Newer separately licensed model variants need their own complete software/model review; a recent model-card update is not an eligible software release. [Source](https://huggingface.co/nvidia/RE-USE)
- **ReconX** · excluded: The README still says the implementation is coming soon. A complete usable released workflow was not established in this pass. [Source](https://github.com/THU-SI/ReconX)
- **Offline Dub Studio** · excluded: Root inspection found README and license only; a released executable implementation was not established. [Source](https://github.com/miku-wwl/offline-dub-studio)
- **AI Animation Engine** · excluded: Available documentation and files did not establish an implemented creative-AI workflow beyond a blueprint/agent-skill collection. [Source](https://github.com/HellKaiser45/ai-animation-engine)
- **Astra Mastering** · excluded: The documented mastering and recommendation path is conventional DSP and rules. Concrete learned-model or agent-driven creative processing was not established. [Source](https://github.com/tradade/astra-mastering)
- **Blob Atelier** · excluded: The SVG mascot generator is procedural; serving as artwork for AI agents does not itself establish an AI-powered creative tool. [Source](https://github.com/victoriawu-77/blob-atelier)
- **Vizport Studio** · excluded: Charting and prompt export were documented, but actual AI-powered visual authoring was not established for the reviewed app. [Source](https://github.com/okorion/vizport-studio)
- **Voxatar** · excluded: The avatar capability is still described as a later project phase, and the initial setup route is incomplete. It is not counted as an available avatar-authoring application. [Source](https://github.com/cmcintosh/voxatar)
- **AiPPT** · excluded: The available documentation mixes project code and a commercial service without establishing a reproducible self-hosted implementation for the advertised workflow. Held from today’s additions. [Source](https://github.com/veasion/AiPPT)
- **AcoustiX** · excluded: The reviewed acoustic data-generation path uses simulation/ray tracing. The linked neural AVR workflow was investigated separately; the simulator alone does not satisfy the AI-only scope. [Source](https://github.com/penn-waves-lab/AcoustiX)
- **TinyLuma** · excluded: Despite its AI-image branding, the documented processing uses bilateral filtering, unsharp masking, LUTs and other conventional algorithms. A learned or agent-driven creative operation was not established. [Source](https://github.com/ThetaCursed/TinyLuma)
